1	# Wan2GP Fork Migration — Design Document
2	
3	## 1. Executive Summary
4	`reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` is currently a flat vendored snapshot with no `.git` directory, so it cannot be cleanly rebased onto `deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP` even though it is roughly 50 days and about 9 minor versions behind. The hottest drift zone is `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`, where 22 files differ and the working diff is roughly 4303 lines.
5	
6	This migration plan is intentionally a planning artifact, not an implementation batch. It breaks the migration into 4 sprints of 2 weeks each so the team can inventory drift first, cut over to a maintainable fork-plus-patch model second, resync the risky `models/ltx2/` surface third, and finish with sustainability controls last.
7	
8	The non-negotiable constraint is that no functionality may be lost during any sprint. The plan therefore treats these as protected behaviors that must survive with explicit verification: IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose, depth, canny, and cameraman modes; the newly landed `ltx_anchor` kind; the existing `ltx_control` pixel cross-fade guide-video path; the `ltx_hybrid` route; `clear_conditioning` keyframe-token stripping before VAE decode; the existing 6-file travel test suite; and the import-path contract that supports both dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers and bare-module callers.
9	
10	## 2. Current-State Inventory
11	
12	### 2a. Vendored tree facts
13	`reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` is currently a plain copied tree rather than a repository checkout, so there is no `.git` metadata to rebase, cherry-pick, or diff-history against. The vendored changelog currently tops out at `WanGP v10.01` dated January 1, 2026, while upstream context for this plan is `v10.951` dated February 19, 2026.
14	
15	The hottest resync zone is `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`, where the working comparison indicates 22 differing files and about 4303 lines of diff. That is large enough that the migration cannot safely be framed as a single blind replacement.
16	
17	The repo also has a non-trivial import and runtime surface already coupled to the current mount path. The approved background for this plan treats that as a 26+ call-site contract, which is why the migration must preserve both filesystem location and import semantics rather than just swapping in a dependency reference.
18	
19	### 2b. Genuinely missing upstream items
20	The current vendored tree is missing several upstream features that are desired outcomes of the migration rather than already-carried drift. Confirmed missing items include `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` and `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json`.
21	
22	Other upstream-side capabilities that this design document should plan to absorb during the migration include `_apply_gamma_to_media`, `LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA`, `LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO`, and the newer prompt-enhancer improvements. These belong in the migration inventory because they are feature gaps, not just cleanup opportunities.
23	
24	### 2c. Already-vendored drift-only items
25	`Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` is already vendored in the current tree. It should therefore be treated as a drift candidate that may need reconciliation with upstream head, not as a missing upstream backfill.
26	
27	That distinction matters for sprint design: Sprint 2 can absorb `self_refiner` drift as a low-risk resync task, while `res2s.py` and `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` remain true additions.
28	
29	### 2d. Patch seam and lifecycle
30	The repo already has an established patch seam for Wan2GP-specific runtime behavior. The module-load trigger is `source/__init__.py:6`, which ensures `source.models.wgp.wgp_patches` is importable during bootstrap.
31	
32	Actual patch application happens later in the runtime lifecycle at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`, where `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` is called after the Wan2GP module is loaded and before orchestration proceeds. The migration plan should preserve this existing seam instead of introducing a second patch package.
33	
34	### 2e. banodoco-carry items
35	Some behaviors are not generic upstream drift and must be treated as load-bearing banodoco carry items until they are either upstreamed or deliberately relocated. The clearest examples are `clear_conditioning` in `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`, the `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` registry in `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py`, and the `ltx_anchor`-related orchestrator rewiring around `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config`, PATH A skip behavior, and segment-local anchor remapping.
36	
37	These items are the reason the migration cannot be framed as “replace vendor tree with upstream head.” Each one needs an explicit carry-location decision later in the plan.
38	
39	### 2f. Pre-existing test coverage gaps
40	The current test suite does not yet pin the cameraman-specific override branch or the task-registry dedup branch. T1 confirmed that current collected cameraman coverage is limited to `test_parse_ltx_control_cameraman` and `test_needs_ic_lora[cameraman-True]`, and neither one reaches `get_ic_lora_entry()`.
41	
42	That leaves the cameraman override branch at `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:310-313` and the dedup logic at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:964-968` unexercised by current tests. Sprint 3 therefore needs new first-class test deliverables rather than relying on `-k` selectors over the existing suite.
43	
44	### 2g. Stale-doc inventory
45	T1 identified existing documentation that still describes `Wan2GP/` as a vendored upstream subtree. Confirmed narrow-sweep hits are `README.md:39`, `STRUCTURE.md:22`, and `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`.
46	
47	The broader sweep also found `STRUCTURE.md:70`, which still describes `Wan2GP/` as “Upstream video generation engine (vendored, DO NOT MODIFY).” Sprint 2 therefore needs a doc-sweep gate that covers both the narrow regex and the broader `Wan2GP` plus `vendor` pass, followed by manual reviewer confirmation because the regex is heuristic.
48	
49	### 2h. Invocation contract
50	The repo’s test and worker invocation contract is `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`, consistent with the locked Python 3.10 environment in `uv.lock` and the worker run examples in `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:195`. This document should treat that as the canonical pytest invocation form for every later verification gate.
51	
52	Plain `pytest` is not sufficient in this repo because it can fail with `ModuleNotFoundError: source` when the expected environment contract is not active. The migration plan therefore needs to standardize on the `uv run --python 3.10` prefix anywhere it names pytest.
53	
54	### 2i. Path-contract verification technique
55	The path contract has two caller classes that must both survive the migration. First, `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` inserts the absolute `Wan2GP/` directory itself into `sys.path`, not the repo root. Second, the more common dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers can work when either the repo root or `Wan2GP/` is on `sys.path`, so they are necessary but not sufficient proof that the mount contract remains correct.
56	
57	The stricter contract is driven by the bare-module callers in `vendor_imports.py:76-88`: `models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, and `models.qwen.qwen_main`. Those class-b callers require the absolute `Wan2GP/` insertion specifically, which is why the Sprint 2 smoke must assert `os.path.abspath(str(get_wan2gp_path())) in [os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path]` with no repo-root fallback.
58	
59	Bare-import reachability should be verified only through filesystem proxies such as `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py')`, `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py')`, and `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py')`. `find_spec` and real imports on `shared.utils.*` are intentionally avoided because `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py` eagerly imports SciPy-sensitive solver modules. Full end-to-end validation of the bare-module callers should then come from the existing test suite exercising `vendor_imports.py` through normal codepaths.
60	
61	## 3. Fork Architecture Decisions
62	The migration will use the hybrid architecture defined by SD-001: `banodoco/Wan2GP` becomes the long-running fork, and `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` becomes a git submodule pinned to that fork rather than a flat copied tree. That choice keeps the existing filesystem contract stable while making future rebases, drift review, and upstream comparison mechanically possible again.
63	
64	Patch ownership is split deliberately rather than informally. SD-002 defines the required triage buckets so every deviation is classified as accept-upstream, upstreamable, banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch, or cruft; SD-003 stages the resync by subsystem so low-risk `shared/` and `postprocessing/` work lands before the hot `models/ltx2/` zone; and SD-004 keeps LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion out of the migration path so the migration is not coupled to a defaults experiment.
65	
66	Feature exposure is also staged. SD-005 keeps `res_2s` and `self_refiner` behind off-by-default flags until the fork relationship is stable, and SD-006 keeps any post-gen detailer work outside this initiative so the migration remains focused on source-of-truth and drift control instead of product expansion.
67	
68	Sustainability is handled as part of the architecture, not as follow-up cleanup. SD-007 sets a monthly rebase cadence with event-triggered syncs on upstream minor-version bumps, while SD-008 adds scheduled drift detection in CI so the repository does not slide back into another opaque vendor snapshot. Together, SD-001 through SD-008 define a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer model rather than a one-time resync.
69	
70	## 4. Sprint Plan (4 × 2 weeks)
71	### Sprint 1 — Triage & Fork Cut (Weeks 1-2)
72	#### Goals
73	- Fork `banodoco/Wan2GP` at upstream HEAD and establish it as the future source of truth.
74	- Build `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` and classify every differing file into the SD-002 buckets.
75	- Pre-classify `clear_conditioning` as bucket `(c)` before any hot-zone resync begins.
76	
77	#### Deliverables
78	- `banodoco/Wan2GP` fork created and available for migration work.
79	- `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with rows covering the 22-file `models/ltx2/` hot zone and every differing file elsewhere.
80	
81	#### Verification Gates
82	- `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` contains at least one row for every differing file and explicit bucket decisions for all 22 `models/ltx2/` files.
83	- The `clear_conditioning` row remains pre-classified as bucket `(c)` with an explicit carry location and verification entry.
84	
85	#### Rollback Plan
86	- Record tag `wan2gp-mig-sprint-1-baseline` before opening the fork workstream.
87	- No `reigh-worker` code changes are planned in this sprint; if the fork cut is rejected, abandon the fork workstream and leave `reigh-worker` at `wan2gp-mig-sprint-1-baseline`.
88	
89	#### Functionality-Preservation Checks

210	## 6. Risk Register
211	| Risk | Sprint | Prob | Impact | Mitigation |
212	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
213	| Triage misclassifies a banodoco-specific hunk as safe upstream drift | S1 | M | H | Spot-check hot-zone rows and require a second-pass review on `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` before Sprint 2 starts |
214	| Silent bare-module caller break: `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` regresses to insert the repo root, so dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers still work while `vendor_imports.py:76-88` callers break | S2 | M | H | Strict path smoke asserts `get_wan2gp_path()` absolute path on `sys.path` with no repo-root fallback, plus filesystem assertions for `Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py`, `Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py`, and `Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py` |
215	| Submodule mount breaks `Wan2GP.*` imports or the `Wan2GP/` filesystem path contract | S2 | M | H | Enforce the SD-001 mount-path invariant and re-run the strict path-contract smoke after cut-over |
216	| `self_refiner` version bump silently changes LTX-2 behavior | S2 | M | M | Compare against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline using the planned LTX-2 smoke |
217	| Stale doc wording survives the cut-over and misleads later maintainers | S2 | L | M | Run both narrow and broad regex sweeps plus a reviewer manual pass, explicitly acknowledging the regex sweep is heuristic |
218	| Verification commands fail the repo environment contract and produce misleading results | Any | M | H | Require `uv run --python 3.10 pytest` for every named pytest invocation |
219	| SciPy trigger via `shared.utils.*` smoke: a verification command imports or `find_spec`s `shared.utils.*` and trips `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py:1-3` | S2 | L | M | Keep the path smoke limited to filesystem checks and `sys.path` assertions only; never use `import` or `find_spec` on `shared.utils.*` |
220	| `clear_conditioning` is silently reverted during the `models/ltx2/` resync | S3 | M | H | Preserve it explicitly as bucket `(c)` carry and gate Sprint 3 on a byte-compare latent fixture |
221	| IC-LoRA registry collides with upstream union-control preload behavior | S3 | H | M | Pin both branches with the new override and fallback tests before accepting the hot-zone resync |
222	| Cameraman override or dedup regression slips through because the current suite never exercised those branches | S3 | H | H | Write `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py` and `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` as first-class Sprint 3 deliverables |
223	| Parallel patch package or split patch registry emerges during consolidation | S4 | M | M | Keep all overrides in `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` per SD-001 and assert a single registry in the patch-state smoke |
224	| Patch smoke is anchored at the wrong lifecycle point and reports false confidence | S4 | M | M | Drive bootstrap to `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` before inspecting patch state |
225	| Monthly rebase cadence slips and drift starts accumulating again | Ongoing | M | M | Use SD-008 weekly drift CI to surface missed cadence before drift becomes opaque again |

231	- Drift CI is green.

233	- The strict path-contract smoke passes, including the bare-import target filesystem assertions.

237	- A subset of subsystems has been synced, but each completed checkpoint still has green tests.
238	- The doc sweeps plus reviewer pass are green.
239	- The strict path smoke is green at the current checkpoint.
240	
241	### Regression observed

243	- Any named regression suite fails.
244	- The path-contract smoke fails either because `get_wan2gp_path()` is not on `sys.path` exactly as required or because any bare-import target filesystem assertion fails.
245	- The doc sweep surfaces stale wording that is not explicitly approved for the current sprint.

247	## 8. Non-Goals
248	- Flipping `ltx_anchor` to default
249	- Real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults
250	- Upscaler-pass anchor re-injection
251	- Identity-drift fixes
252	
253	## 9. Settled Decisions
254	### Decision 1
255	- `id`: `SD-001`
256	- `load_bearing`: `true`
257	- `decision`: Use a hybrid fork shape: maintain `banodoco/Wan2GP` as the long-running fork and consume it in `reigh-worker` as a git submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`.
258	  Path/import contract sub-clause:
259	  - The submodule mount path stays exactly `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`; no relocation or rename.
260	  - `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` must continue inserting the absolute `Wan2GP/` directory itself into `sys.path`, not the repo root or a parent/sibling path.
261	  - The bare-module callers in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py:76-88` must remain valid, explicitly including `models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, and `models.qwen.qwen_main`.
262	  - The dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers remain supported as the other caller class.
263	  - Deviations that do not belong in the fork stay in the existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` seam, which is module-loaded via `source/__init__.py:6` and applied by `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`.
264	  - No parallel runtime-side Wan2GP patch package is introduced.
265	- `rationale`: This preserves the current runtime contract while restoring maintainability. The stable mount path protects the import surface already used by both dotted `Wan2GP.*` imports and the bare-module import sites, and the existing patch seam prevents fork-only changes from being scattered into a second, conflicting patch registry.
266	
267	### Decision 2
268	- `id`: `SD-002`
269	- `load_bearing`: `true`
270	- `decision`: Every deviation between the current vendored tree and upstream is triaged into one of four buckets: `(a) accept-upstream`, `(b) upstreamable`, `(c) banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch`, `(d) cruft`. The canonical artifact is `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with columns `path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification`.
271	  Illustrative sample row:
272	
273	  ```csv
274	  path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification
275	  Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py,"clear_conditioning() strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode",banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch,banodoco/Wan2GP or source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py,worker-runtime,byte-compare saved latent fixture
276	  ```
277	- `rationale`: The migration risk is concentrated in drift classification, not in git mechanics alone. A forced bucket per hunk makes carry decisions reviewable, gives Sprint 1 a concrete deliverable, and prevents load-bearing customizations like `clear_conditioning` from being lost inside an undifferentiated merge.
278	
279	### Decision 3
280	- `id`: `SD-003`
281	- `load_bearing`: `true`
282	- `decision`: Resync by subsystem, not all at once: `shared/` plus `postprocessing/` first, `models/ltx2/` second, and defaults/configs last.
283	- `rationale`: The repo already depends on fragile `models/ltx2/` behavior, including `ltx_anchor`, IC-LoRA handling, and `clear_conditioning`. Staging the migration lowers blast radius, lets the team validate path/import and doc updates before touching the 22-file hot zone, and keeps rollback boundaries understandable.
284	
285	### Decision 4
286	- `id`: `SD-004`
287	- `load_bearing`: `false`
288	- `decision`: Hold LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion for a separate A/B sprint outside the migration, even if `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` is imported during the resync.
289	- `rationale`: Default promotion is a product-choice experiment, not a prerequisite for establishing the fork relationship. Separating it from the migration keeps “source sync” and “new default behavior” from failing together or obscuring regressions.
290	
291	### Decision 5
292	- `id`: `SD-005`
293	- `load_bearing`: `false`
294	- `decision`: Introduce `res_2s` and refreshed `self_refiner` support behind off-by-default flags during Sprints 2 and 3, with any default promotion deferred until upstream behavior and local verification converge.
295	- `rationale`: Both features are desirable, but neither is required to complete the vendor-to-fork transition safely. Flagging them keeps the migration forward-moving without forcing immediate behavior changes in the travel pipeline.
296	
297	### Decision 6
298	- `id`: `SD-006`
299	- `load_bearing`: `false`
300	- `decision`: Keep post-gen detailer work out of this migration plan and treat it as a separate initiative after the fork-plus-patch relationship is stable.
301	- `rationale`: The user preference already points toward a task-handler implementation rather than inline travel-pipeline detailer logic. Folding that feature into the migration would expand scope, confuse verification ownership, and make it harder to distinguish migration regressions from brand-new behavior.
302	
303	### Decision 7
304	- `id`: `SD-007`
305	- `load_bearing`: `true`
306	- `decision`: Rebase the fork monthly, with additional event-triggered syncs whenever upstream ships a new minor version that touches relevant subsystems.
307	- `rationale`: Weekly rebases would create churn without matching the repo’s current staffing and risk profile, while ad hoc syncs are what created the current opaque drift. Monthly cadence plus event triggers is frequent enough to keep diffs reviewable and infrequent enough to be realistic.
308	
309	### Decision 8
310	- `id`: `SD-008`
311	- `load_bearing`: `true`
312	- `decision`: Add `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` as a weekly scheduled CI job that compares the pinned submodule pointer against upstream HEAD and reports drift for review.
313	- `rationale`: Once the vendor tree becomes a submodule, drift becomes measurable. A scheduled CI check converts that measurability into an operating habit, so the repo does not return to a silent `.git`-less copy that is only audited after major divergence has already accumulated.
314

1	"""Vendor-facing imports isolated behind a runtime boundary."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import sys
6	from importlib import import_module
7	from typing import Any
8	
9	_BRIDGE_MODULE_NAME = ".".join(("source", "runtime", "wgp_bridge"))
10	
11	
12	def _get_bridge_module():
13	    bridge_module = sys.modules.get("source.runtime.wgp_bridge")
14	    if bridge_module is None:
15	        bridge_module = import_module(_BRIDGE_MODULE_NAME)
16	    return bridge_module

18

21	    ensure_fn = getattr(bridge_module, "ensure_wan2gp_on_path")
22	    return ensure_fn()
23	
24	
25	def _import_primary(module_name: str):
26	    bridge_module = _get_bridge_module()

28	    _ensure_bridge_bootstrap()

"""Runtime path helpers for Wan2GP bootstrap."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

from source.runtime.process_globals import get_bootstrap_controller, run_bootstrap_once


def get_repo_root() -> Path:
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def get_wan2gp_path() -> Path:
    return get_repo_root() / "Wan2GP"


def ensure_wan2gp_on_path() -> str:
    wan2gp_path = str(get_wan2gp_path().resolve())

    def _initializer() -> None:
        if wan2gp_path in sys.path:
            sys.path.remove(wan2gp_path)
        sys.path.insert(0, wan2gp_path)

    run_bootstrap_once(
        "wan2gp.sys_path",
        _initializer,
        version="2026-03-16",
        controller=get_bootstrap_controller("wan2gp.paths"),
    )
    return wan2gp_path
```

```
1	# Reigh-Worker
2	
3	GPU worker for [Reigh](https://github.com/banodoco/Reigh), for running locally or in the cloud via [Reigh Worker Orchestrator](https://github.com/banodoco/Reigh-Worker-Orchestrator/) — processes video generation tasks using [Wan2GP](https://github.com/deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP).
4	
5	## Quick Start
6	
7	```bash
8	# 1. Linux baseline: Ubuntu 22.04/24.04 with NVIDIA drivers, ffmpeg, git, curl
9	#    Ubuntu 24.04+ needs Python 3.10 from deadsnakes before the command below works:
10	#    sudo add-apt-repository ppa:deadsnakes/ppa && sudo apt-get update
11	
12	# 2. Bootstrap uv once
13	curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh
14	export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"
15	
16	# 3. Sync the locked environment from the repo root
17	uv sync --locked --python 3.10 --extra cuda124
18	
19	# 4. Run the worker
20	uv run --python 3.10 python run_worker.py \
21	  [REDACTED] \
22	  --wgp-profile 4 \
23	  --idle-release-minutes 15
24	```
25	
26	Get credentials from [reigh.art](https://reigh.art/).
27	
28	The Reigh app now generates two command tabs:
29	- `Install`: bootstrap or force-resync at the configured `Worker repo location`
30	- `Run`: the normal day-to-day launch path, which still runs `uv sync` before starting
31	
32	Both commands always `cd` into the configured repo path first so copy-pasting from a fresh terminal in your home directory still works.
33	
34	## Packaging Notes
35	
36	- `pyproject.toml` is the canonical dependency definition for the worker runtime.
37	- `requirements.txt` remains committed as rollback ballast during the rollout.
38	- `uv.lock` is expected to be refreshed from the worker repo root with Python 3.10.
39	- `Wan2GP/requirements.txt` is still vendored upstream input. Short-term drift between the subtree and the root lock is acceptable only until the next planned lock refresh; if upstream requirements change, update the mirrored root dependency set and regenerate the lock.
40	
41	## Rollback
42	
43	- There is no runtime pip fallback on the migrated branch. Failed `uv sync` runs should fail loudly.
44	- If the very first uv-based launch fails after migrating an existing machine, restore the newest `venv.pre-uv-*` or `.venv.pre-uv-*` backup back to `venv/` or `.venv/`, remove `.uv-migrated`, and investigate from there.
45	- If the release itself must be rolled back, revert the uv rollout commits and return to the pre-uv revision that still bootstraps from the committed `requirements.txt`.
46	
47	## Standalone Usage
48	
49	Use the generation engine without Reigh for local testing or custom pipelines:
50	
51	```bash
52	# Join two video clips with AI-generated transition
53	python examples/join_clips_example.py \
54	    --clip1 scene1.mp4 --clip2 scene2.mp4 \
55	    --output transition.mp4 --prompt "smooth camera glide"
56	
57	# Regenerate corrupted frames
58	python examples/inpaint_frames_example.py \
59	    --video my_video.mp4 --start-frame 45 --end-frame 61 \
60	    --output fixed.mp4 --prompt "smooth motion"
61	```
62	
63	### Using HeadlessTaskQueue Directly
64	
65	```python
66	from headless_model_management import HeadlessTaskQueue, GenerationTask
67	from pathlib import Path
68	
69	queue = HeadlessTaskQueue(wan_dir=str(Path(__file__).parent / "Wan2GP"), max_workers=1)
70	queue.start()
71	
72	task = GenerationTask(
73	    id="my_task",
74	    model="wan_2_2_vace_lightning_baseline_2_2_2",
75	    prompt="a cat walking through a garden",
76	    parameters={"video_length": 81, "resolution": "896x512", "seed": 42}
77	)
78	
79	queue.submit_task(task)
80	result = queue.wait_for_completion(task.id, timeout=600)
81	print(f"Output: {result.get('output_path')}" if result.get("success") else f"Error: {result.get('error')}")
82	
83	queue.stop()
84	```
85	
86	## Debugging
87	
88	```bash
89	uv run --python 3.10 python -m debug task <task_id>          # Investigate a task
90	uv run --python 3.10 python -m debug tasks --status Failed   # List recent failures
91	```
92	
93	## Code Health
94	
95	<img src="scorecard.png" width="800">
96	
97	## Project Structure
98	
99	See [STRUCTURE.md](STRUCTURE.md) for detailed project layout.
100	
101	## Powered By
102	
103	[Wan2GP](https://github.com/deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP) by [deepbeepmeep](https://github.com/deepbeepmeep) — the `Wan2GP/` directory contains the upstream engine.
104
```

```
1	# Project Structure
2	
3	Queue-based video generation system built on [Wan2GP](https://github.com/deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP).
4	
5	## Core Files
6	
7	```
8	├── pyproject.toml               # Canonical uv project metadata
9	├── uv.lock                      # Locked Python 3.10 dependency graph
10	├── requirements.txt             # Rollback ballast during uv rollout
11	├── worker.py                    # Main worker - polls DB, claims tasks, routes to handlers
12	├── headless_model_management.py # Thin facade re-exporting HeadlessTaskQueue
13	├── headless_wgp.py              # Thin facade re-exporting WanOrchestrator
14	├── heartbeat_guardian.py        # Process-level heartbeat monitoring
15	```
16	
17	## Runtime Model
18	
19	- Runtime commands now assume `uv sync --locked --python 3.10` from the repo root on every launch.
20	- `uv run --python 3.10 ...` is the supported execution path for the worker and debug tools.
21	- Local Linux installs are validated against Ubuntu 22.04 as the baseline. Ubuntu 24.04+ requires Python 3.10 packages from deadsnakes before sync can succeed.
22	- `Wan2GP/` remains vendored upstream. Dependency drift inside that subtree is tolerated only until the next lock refresh; any upstream requirement change must be mirrored back into the root project metadata before regenerating `uv.lock`.
23	- Rollback stays repo-based: there is no runtime pip fallback on the uv branch. First-migration failures restore the timestamped `venv.pre-uv-*` or `.venv.pre-uv-*` backup, while release rollback means reverting to the pre-uv revision that still uses `requirements.txt`.
24	
25	## source/ Package
26	
27	Organized into 5 top-level groups:
28	
29	### source/core/ — Infrastructure
30	- `db/` — Database operations (Supabase): config, task claim/completion/status/dependencies, edge helpers
31	- `log/` — Structured logging: core logger, database log sink, safe formatting, timing
32	- `params/` — Typed parameter handling: TaskConfig, LoRAConfig, phase_config, structure guidance
33	- `constants.py` — Shared constants
34	- `platform_utils.py` — Platform-specific utilities (ALSA suppression, headless env)
35	
36	### source/media/ — Media Processing
37	- `video/` — Video operations: ffmpeg, crossfade, color matching, frame extraction, hires, VACE frames
38	- `structure/` — Structure guidance video: generation, download, preprocessing, segment tracking
39	- `visualization/` — Debug visualizations: comparison grids, layouts, timeline
40	- `vlm/` — Vision-language model: prompt generation, image prep
41	
42	### source/models/ — Model Integration
43	- `wgp/` — WanOrchestrator: parameter resolution, model ops, LoRA setup, generation strategies, patches
44	- `comfy/` — ComfyUI integration
45	- `lora/` — LoRA download, path resolution, collision handling
46	- `model_handlers/` — Task-type-specific handlers (QwenHandler for 7 Qwen image task types)
47	
48	### source/task_handlers/ — Task Orchestration
49	- `tasks/` — Task routing: registry, type definitions, DB→GenerationTask conversion
50	- `queue/` — HeadlessTaskQueue: task queue, task processor, download ops, lifecycle
51	- `travel/` — Multi-image travel: orchestrator, segment processor, SVI config, chaining, stitch
52	- `join/` — Video clip joining: orchestrator, generation, final stitch, VLM enhancement
53	- `worker/` — Worker utilities: heartbeat, fatal error handling, debug printing
54	- Top-level handlers: `edit_video_orchestrator.py`, `magic_edit.py`, `inpaint_frames.py`, `extract_frame.py`, `rife_interpolate.py`, `create_visualization.py`
55	
56	### source/utils/ — Shared Utilities
57	- `download_utils.py` — File/image download helpers
58	- `frame_utils.py` — Frame manipulation (pose, keypoint detection)
59	- `resolution_utils.py` — Resolution parsing and grid snapping
60	- `prompt_utils.py` — Prompt validation and formatting
61	- `mask_utils.py` — Mask generation for VACE
62	- `output_path_utils.py` — Output directory management
63	- `lora_validation.py` — LoRA file validation
64	
65	### source/db_operations.py — Database Facade
66	Thin facade with `_ConfigProxy` for runtime config propagation. Delegates to `source/core/db/`.
67	
68	## External
69	
70	- `Wan2GP/` — Upstream video generation engine (vendored, DO NOT MODIFY)
71	- `debug/` — CLI tool for investigating tasks/workers (`python -m debug`)
72	- `scripts/` — Standalone utilities (test task creation, LoRA rank conversion)
73	
74	## Data Flow
75	
76	```
77	DB → worker.py → TaskRegistry → HeadlessTaskQueue → WanOrchestrator → wgp.py → Files
78	```
79	
80	1. **worker.py** polls tasks table, claims work
81	2. **TaskRegistry** routes task_type to the appropriate handler
82	3. **HeadlessTaskQueue** manages model loading, LoRA downloads, and task processing
83	4. **WanOrchestrator** maps parameters, calls WGP
84	5. **wgp.py** performs generation, writes to outputs/
85	6. Results flow back through the chain to update DB
86	
87	## Database
88	
89	| Column | Purpose |
90	|--------|---------|
91	| `id` | UUID primary key |
92	| `task_type` | e.g., `vace`, `travel_orchestrator`, `t2v` |
93	| `dependant_on` | Optional FK forming execution DAG |
94	| `params` | JSON payload |
95	| `status` | `Queued` → `In Progress` → `Complete`/`Failed` |
96	| `output_location` | Final output path/URL |
97	
98	## Configuration
99	
100	| Variable | Purpose |
101	|----------|---------|
102	| `SUPABASE_URL` | Supabase project URL |
103	| `SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY` | Service role key |
104	| `--db-type` | `sqlite` (default) or `supabase` |
105	| `--debug` | Keep temp folders, extra logs |
106
```

190	## Debug Logging
191	
192	Enable SVI debug logs with `--debug` flag:

195	uv run --python 3.10 python worker.py --debug --supabase-url ... --supabase-access-token ...

198	Look for `[SVI_DEBUG]` prefix in logs.
199	
200	## Dependency Drift Note
201	
202	`Wan2GP/` is still a vendored upstream subtree. If kijai-side dependency requirements change, do not edit the worker runtime ad hoc inside the pod. Mirror the requirement change back into the root worker project metadata, regenerate `uv.lock`, and then let the normal `uv sync --locked --python 3.10` launch path pick it up. Temporary subtree drift is acceptable only until that lock refresh lands.
203	
204	If a migration rollback is needed while debugging dependency changes, keep the same repo-level rule: do not add a runtime pip fallback on the uv branch. Restore the timestamped `*.pre-uv-*` environment backup for a first-migration failure, or revert the rollout commits to return to the older `requirements.txt` bootstrap.
205	
206	## Common Issues
207	
208	### svi2pro Not Taking Effect
209	
210	**Symptom:** SVI path not taken even when `svi2pro=True` is passed in task params.
211	
212	**Cause:** The `svi2pro` flag must be in the **model_def**, not just generation params. It's read at `model_def.get("svi2pro", False)`.
213	
214	**Solution:** Any of:
215	1. Use a model that has `"svi2pro": true` in its JSON config (e.g., `wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_svi_3_3`)
216	2. Pass `svi2pro=True` in task params - **now automatically patched into model_def at runtime**
217	
218	### How Runtime svi2pro Patching Works
219

> AGENT

Got enough context. Writing a concrete phased plan now.

## Key evidence gathered

- **Current state**: `Wan2GP/` is tracked in `reigh-worker` (1400 files committed), no `.git` subdirectory. Existing remote `upstream = deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP`, `origin = banodoco/reigh-worker`.
- **Triage CSV**: 269 content rows (line 1 = header). Contains shared/ and postprocessing/ bucket decisions but **no row for `shared/utils/self_refiner.py`** — plan §2c treats it as separate drift-only resync. No row for `postprocessing/` subtree (present in disk) — will default to accept-upstream.
- **Path contract**: `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` at `source/core/runtime_paths.py:20-32` already inserts absolute `Wan2GP/` onto `sys.path[0]`. Bare-module callers at `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py:76-88` rely on that. Smoke must succeed post-cutover without modification to runtime_paths.py.
- **Scope tension**: idea says "NON-NEGOTIABLE: Sprint 2 does NOT touch any files under `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`" but also says "Introduce `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`". Treating this as add-only (new file, bucket-a per triage line 110) — no existing ltx2 file is modified.
- **Fork content strategy**: because Sprint 2 touches only shared/ + postprocessing/ (plus res2s + self_refiner), the banodoco fork branch must carry **all current banodoco drift outside those subsystems** (models/ltx2/, models/wan/, defaults/, profiles/, loras_qwen/, etc.) so submoduling doesn't silently revert unrelated banodoco-carry items.
- **Existing tags**: `wan2gp-mig-sprint-1-baseline` already exists.

## Plan

````md
# Implementation Plan: Wan2GP Fork Migration — Sprint 2

## Overview

Sprint 2 replaces `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` (a 1400-file flat-copied tree, currently tracked in reigh-worker git) with a git submodule mounted at the same path, pointing at a branch on `banodoco/Wan2GP` whose working tree matches current banodoco behavior everywhere **except** the explicitly-resynced surfaces. Four concrete outputs:

1. Submodule cutover that preserves the SD-001 path contract (absolute `Wan2GP/` on `sys.path`, bare-module callers still resolve).
2. `shared/` + `postprocessing/` resync honoring `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` bucket decisions.
3. `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` introduced from upstream HEAD (available-only, not promoted to default per SD-005). `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` aligned to upstream HEAD.
4. Stale-doc wording removed from `README.md:39`, `STRUCTURE.md:22`, `STRUCTURE.md:70`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`.

Sprint 2 must NOT touch existing files under `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`. `res2s.py` is a new file added to that tree, which is consistent because the non-negotiable is about modifying existing ltx2 behavior, not about the directory being frozen. No behavior change outside `shared/` + `postprocessing/` drift classified as bucket (a).

Fork construction strategy: build an integration branch `reigh-sprint-2` on `banodoco/Wan2GP` whose tree equals `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` everywhere (preserving all current banodoco drift — defaults, models/wan/, models/ltx2/, loras_qwen/, profiles/, plugins/, etc.) EXCEPT where Sprint 2 is explicitly resyncing. The submodule then pins to that branch tip SHA. This keeps non-Sprint-2 surfaces byte-identical to pre-cutover, and centralizes Sprint 2's tiny actual delta into the `shared/` + `postprocessing/` + self_refiner + res2s changes.

## Phase 0: Preflight

### Step 1: Record baseline tag and capture pre-cutover fingerprint
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** working tree is clean (expected untracked only: `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md`, `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv`, `scripts/live_test/`). Confirm `HEAD == 9656cc90` or current `main` head.
2. **Create annotated tag** `wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline` on `reigh-worker` HEAD: `git tag -a wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline -m "Sprint 2 baseline"`. Do NOT push yet — tag lives locally until Sprint 2 lands.
3. **Fingerprint current Wan2GP tree** for post-cutover byte-compare verification (paths outside Sprint 2's touch set should be unchanged): `git ls-tree -r HEAD Wan2GP | sha256sum > /tmp/wan2gp_pre_cutover.sha256` and capture tree SHAs for `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/` and `Wan2GP/models/qwen/` specifically for the non-regression assertion.
4. **Verify fork access**: confirm `banodoco/Wan2GP` is reachable and that `main` points at a commit equal to (or descended from) upstream `deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP` HEAD as of Sprint 1. Capture banodoco `main` tip SHA and upstream HEAD SHA.

## Phase 1: Construct the `reigh-sprint-2` fork branch

### Step 2: Clone the banodoco fork into a scratch directory (`/tmp/wan2gp-fork/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Clone**: `git clone https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git /tmp/wan2gp-fork` and `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork fetch origin main`.
2. **Branch from `main`**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout -b reigh-sprint-2 origin/main`. The starting point is upstream HEAD (Sprint 1 state).
3. **Confirm** tip SHA matches Sprint 1's fork cut.

### Step 3: Mirror current banodoco vendored tree onto `reigh-sprint-2` (everything-carry commit)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Compute the target content** for the fork branch: it must equal `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` as-of baseline (the current flat tree). This single copy preserves all current banodoco drift — defaults, `models/ltx2/` (hot zone, not touched until Sprint 3), `models/wan/`, `models/qwen/`, `loras_qwen/`, `profiles/`, `plugins/`, `preprocessing/`, `shared/`, `postprocessing/`, top-level `pyproject.toml`/`requirements.txt`, etc.
2. **Rsync-style copy**: from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` into `/tmp/wan2gp-fork/`, excluding `.git*` and any ignored artifacts. Use `rsync -a --delete --exclude='.git' --exclude='__pycache__' --exclude='.gitignore-override' /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ /tmp/wan2gp-fork/` (keep the fork's existing `.gitignore` from upstream, do not overwrite it with the reigh-worker one; instead preserve the already-in-fork `.gitignore` by checking out `git checkout main -- .gitignore` after the rsync if needed).
3. **Stage + review diff**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork add -A` then `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork status` and spot-check that the staged changes match the roughly 22 `models/ltx2/` file diffs, banodoco-only files in `defaults/`, `loras_qwen/`, etc., and expected bucket (b)+(c) drift everywhere else.
4. **Commit**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork commit -m "banodoco carry: mirror reigh-worker/Wan2GP baseline onto fork"`. This single commit converts the fork branch from upstream-HEAD-only to banodoco-baseline-equivalent.

### Step 4: Apply Sprint 2 resync delta on top of the carry commit
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`shared/` triage application**: for every row in `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with path under `Wan2GP/shared/`:
   - **Bucket (a) `accept-upstream`**: revert to upstream content. `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- <path>` for each bucket-a file (these are the files where banodoco had drift we now accept back to upstream; for upstream-only-addition rows, the file is already in `origin/main` and the carry commit may not have touched it — double-check by grepping CSV).
   - **Bucket (b) `upstreamable`**: leave as carry commit (banodoco content preserved). No action.
   - **Bucket (c) `banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch`**: leave as carry commit.
   - **Bucket (d) `cruft`**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork rm <path>`. (None expected in `shared/` per current CSV; verify.)
2. **`postprocessing/` triage application**: grep the CSV for `^Wan2GP/postprocessing/`. Current CSV has **no** `postprocessing/` rows, which implies no drift vs upstream — the carry commit's postprocessing contents already equal upstream. No action needed, but verify by running `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork diff origin/main -- postprocessing/` — expected: empty diff. If non-empty, flag and treat each divergence as bucket (a) resync to upstream unless there is a positive carry reason (ask the reviewer).
3. **`shared/utils/self_refiner.py` alignment**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- shared/utils/self_refiner.py`. Plan §2c treats this as a drift-only resync to upstream HEAD (CSV has no row because it's neither missing upstream nor carry). Stage.
4. **`models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` introduction**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`. This is CSV row 110 — upstream-only addition, bucket (a). Stage. No wiring change is needed: the file is a sampler implementation that is only invoked when a defaults/profile references `res_2s`. The `HQ (Res2s).json` preset already exists (CSV row 195, bucket a), so availability is complete without promotion to default. SD-005 gating is satisfied because no defaults file is modified to make `res_2s` the default sampler.
5. **Commit the Sprint 2 delta**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork commit -m "sprint-2: resync shared/ + postprocessing/, introduce res2s, align self_refiner"`. Verify the commit diff is confined to `shared/`, `postprocessing/`, `shared/utils/self_refiner.py` (already under shared), and `models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`.
6. **Push** `reigh-sprint-2` to `banodoco/Wan2GP`: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork push -u origin reigh-sprint-2`. (Confirm with user before pushing since this is a visible-to-banodoco action.)
7. **Capture tip SHA** for the submodule pointer.

## Phase 2: Reigh-worker submodule cutover

### Step 5: Replace flat `Wan2GP/` tree with submodule (`reigh-worker/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Remove tracked flat tree**: `git rm -r --cached Wan2GP/` (removes from index, leaves working copy). Then `rm -rf Wan2GP/` to remove the physical directory. Do NOT delete `docs/` or anything else.
2. **Add submodule**: `git submodule add -b reigh-sprint-2 https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git Wan2GP`. This creates `.gitmodules`, populates `.git/modules/Wan2GP/`, and checks out the branch tip into `Wan2GP/`.
3. **Pin the submodule pointer** to the specific SHA from Step 4.7 (not just the branch name) so the submodule is reproducible: `git -C Wan2GP checkout <sha-from-step-4.7>` then `git add Wan2GP .gitmodules`.
4. **Verify `.gitmodules` content**:
   ```
   [submodule "Wan2GP"]
       path = Wan2GP
       url = https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git
       branch = reigh-sprint-2
   ```
5. **Verify submodule health**: `git submodule status` shows `Wan2GP` at the pinned SHA with no `+` or `-` prefix.
6. **Do not commit yet** — run validation gates first, then commit once green.

### Step 6: Validate path-contract smoke (`strict path smoke`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the strict path-contract one-liner from the repo root:
   ```bash
   python3 -c "import os, sys; from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path, get_wan2gp_path; ensure_wan2gp_on_path(); assert os.path.abspath(str(get_wan2gp_path())) in [os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path]; assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py'); print('path-contract smoke OK')"
   ```
2. **Must exit 0** with `path-contract smoke OK` on stdout. If any assertion fails, pause cutover and diagnose (most likely: fork branch missing one of the five listed files; fix in Phase 1 and re-sync submodule pointer).

### Step 7: Run the 6-file travel regression suite
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run**:
   ```bash
   uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py
   ```
2. **Must be all-green**. If any test fails, inspect whether the resynced `shared/` file touches code on the travel path. If so, reclassify the triggering file to bucket (c) on the fork branch (carry banodoco drift), amend Phase 1 Step 4, re-push, and re-pin.

## Phase 3: Doc updates

### Step 8: Rewrite stale vendored-subtree wording (`reigh-worker/README.md`, `reigh-worker/STRUCTURE.md`, `reigh-worker/docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`README.md:39`** — replace the "vendored upstream input ... subtree" sentence with submodule-accurate language, e.g.:
   > `Wan2GP/requirements.txt` lives inside the `Wan2GP/` git submodule (pinned to `banodoco/Wan2GP`). Dependency alignment should be done by bumping the submodule pointer and mirroring upstream requirement changes into the root worker `pyproject.toml`, then regenerating `uv.lock`.
2. **`README.md:103`** (last line, "Powered By" section) — replace "the `Wan2GP/` directory contains the upstream engine" with "mounted as a git submodule at `Wan2GP/`, pinned to `banodoco/Wan2GP`." if present.
3. **`STRUCTURE.md:22`** — replace `vendored upstream. Dependency drift ...` with submodule-accurate language describing pointer bumps rather than subtree edits.

5. **`docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`** — replace `still a vendored upstream subtree` with submodule language. Preserve the paragraph's existing guidance about mirroring requirements into root worker metadata; just correct the mechanism.

### Step 9: Doc-sweep gate (`reigh-worker/docs/`, `reigh-worker/README.md`, `reigh-worker/STRUCTURE.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Narrow sweep must return zero hits**:
   ```bash
   rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md
   ```
2. **Broader sweep must return zero hits**:
   ```bash
   rg -l 'Wan2GP' docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md | xargs -I{} rg -H -n 'DO NOT MODIFY' {}
   ```
3. Neither command should print any lines. Note: both hits on `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md` for the first regex are historical prose describing the pre-Sprint-2 state; those are expected to remain (the plan doc is not among the stale-doc update targets and its narrative is about what *was* true). Confirm with the reviewer that those hits are acceptable — if not, reword those prose lines in the plan doc too.

## Phase 4: Land

### Step 10: Commit the submodule cutover + doc updates
**Scope:** Small
1. **Stage**: `.gitmodules`, `Wan2GP` (the gitlink), all deletions under `Wan2GP/`, `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`.
2. **Commit** with a message along the lines of `chore: replace vendored Wan2GP tree with submodule at reigh-sprint-2`. Use Sprint 2 wording and reference the baseline tag in the body.
3. **Do not push without explicit user confirmation** — the submodule commit permanently alters the repo shape.

## Execution Order

1. Preflight and baseline tag first (Step 1) — this is the rollback anchor.
2. Build the fork branch completely offline in a scratch clone (Steps 2–4), push only after local sanity checks.
3. Cut reigh-worker over to the submodule (Step 5), then gate on the strict path smoke (Step 6) BEFORE running pytest. Path smoke is cheap; pytest is slow.
4. Run the 6-file travel suite (Step 7).
5. Update docs (Step 8) and gate doc-sweep (Step 9).
6. Commit (Step 10).

## Validation Order

1. Cheapest: strict path-contract smoke (Step 6) — instant feedback on the SD-001 invariant.
2. Fast: doc-sweep regex sweeps (Step 9).
3. Slowest: 6-file travel pytest suite (Step 7).

If any of (A) strict path smoke, (B) 6-file suite, (C) doc-sweep gate fails, roll back per the idea's rollback plan:
```bash
git submodule deinit Wan2GP && git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline
```
Rollback leaves the banodoco `reigh-sprint-2` branch in place (no harm); push can be retried after fixing the failing gate.

        Current plan (markdown):
        # Implementation Plan: Wan2GP Fork Migration — Sprint 2

## Overview

Sprint 2 replaces `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` (a 1400-file flat-copied tree, currently tracked in reigh-worker git) with a git submodule mounted at the same path, pointing at a branch on `banodoco/Wan2GP` whose working tree matches current banodoco behavior everywhere **except** the explicitly-resynced surfaces. Four concrete outputs:

1. Submodule cutover that preserves the SD-001 path contract (absolute `Wan2GP/` on `sys.path`, bare-module callers still resolve).
2. `shared/` + `postprocessing/` resync honoring `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` bucket decisions.
3. `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` introduced from upstream HEAD (available-only, not promoted to default per SD-005). `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` aligned to upstream HEAD.
4. Stale-doc wording removed from `README.md:39`, `STRUCTURE.md:22`, `STRUCTURE.md:70`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`.

Sprint 2 must NOT touch existing files under `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`. `res2s.py` is a new file added to that tree, which is consistent because the non-negotiable is about modifying existing ltx2 behavior, not about the directory being frozen. No behavior change outside `shared/` + `postprocessing/` drift classified as bucket (a).

Fork construction strategy: build an integration branch `reigh-sprint-2` on `banodoco/Wan2GP` whose tree equals `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` everywhere (preserving all current banodoco drift — defaults, models/wan/, models/ltx2/, loras_qwen/, profiles/, plugins/, etc.) EXCEPT where Sprint 2 is explicitly resyncing. The submodule then pins to that branch tip SHA. This keeps non-Sprint-2 surfaces byte-identical to pre-cutover, and centralizes Sprint 2's tiny actual delta into the `shared/` + `postprocessing/` + self_refiner + res2s changes.

## Phase 0: Preflight

### Step 1: Record baseline tag and capture pre-cutover fingerprint
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** working tree is clean (expected untracked only: `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md`, `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv`, `scripts/live_test/`). Confirm HEAD is current `main`.
2. **Create annotated tag** `wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline` on `reigh-worker` HEAD: `git tag -a wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline -m "Sprint 2 baseline"`. Do NOT push yet — tag lives locally until Sprint 2 lands.
3. **Fingerprint current Wan2GP tree** for post-cutover byte-compare verification: `git ls-tree -r HEAD Wan2GP | sha256sum > /tmp/wan2gp_pre_cutover.sha256` and capture tree SHAs for `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/` and `Wan2GP/models/qwen/` specifically for the non-regression assertion.
4. **Verify fork access**: confirm `banodoco/Wan2GP` is reachable and capture banodoco `main` tip SHA + upstream HEAD SHA.

## Phase 1: Construct the `reigh-sprint-2` fork branch

### Step 2: Clone the banodoco fork into a scratch directory (`/tmp/wan2gp-fork/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Clone**: `git clone https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git /tmp/wan2gp-fork` and `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork fetch origin main`.
2. **Branch from `main`**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout -b reigh-sprint-2 origin/main`. The starting point is upstream HEAD (Sprint 1 state).
3. **Confirm** tip SHA matches Sprint 1's fork cut.

### Step 3: Mirror current banodoco vendored tree onto `reigh-sprint-2` (everything-carry commit)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Compute the target content** for the fork branch: it must equal `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` as-of baseline. This preserves all current banodoco drift — defaults, `models/ltx2/` (hot zone, not touched until Sprint 3), `models/wan/`, `models/qwen/`, `loras_qwen/`, `profiles/`, `plugins/`, `preprocessing/`, `shared/`, `postprocessing/`, top-level `pyproject.toml`/`requirements.txt`, etc.
2. **Rsync-style copy**: from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` into `/tmp/wan2gp-fork/`, excluding `.git*` and any ignored artifacts.
3. **Stage + review diff**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork add -A` then `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork status` and spot-check that the staged changes match the ~22 `models/ltx2/` file diffs, banodoco-only files in `defaults/`, `loras_qwen/`, etc., and expected bucket (b)+(c) drift everywhere else.
4. **Commit**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork commit -m "banodoco carry: mirror reigh-worker/Wan2GP baseline onto fork"`. This single commit converts the fork branch from upstream-HEAD-only to banodoco-baseline-equivalent.

### Step 4: Apply Sprint 2 resync delta on top of the carry commit
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`shared/` triage application**: for every row in `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with path under `Wan2GP/shared/`:
   - **Bucket (a) `accept-upstream`**: revert to upstream content via `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- <path>` (these are files where banodoco had drift we now accept back to upstream).
   - **Bucket (b) `upstreamable`**: leave as carry commit (banodoco content preserved). No action.
   - **Bucket (c) `banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch`**: leave as carry commit.
   - **Bucket (d) `cruft`**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork rm <path>`. (None expected in `shared/` per current CSV; verify.)
2. **`postprocessing/` triage application**: current CSV has no `postprocessing/` rows, implying no drift vs upstream. Verify `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork diff origin/main -- postprocessing/` is empty. If non-empty, flag and treat each divergence as bucket (a) resync unless reviewer directs otherwise.
3. **`shared/utils/self_refiner.py` alignment**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- shared/utils/self_refiner.py`. Plan §2c treats this as drift-only resync. Stage.
4. **`models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` introduction**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`. CSV row 110 — upstream-only addition, bucket (a). No wiring change: the file is a sampler implementation only invoked when a defaults/profile references `res_2s`. `HQ (Res2s).json` preset already exists (CSV row 195). SD-005 gating is satisfied because no defaults file is modified to make `res_2s` the default sampler.
5. **Commit the Sprint 2 delta**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork commit -m "sprint-2: resync shared/ + postprocessing/, introduce res2s, align self_refiner"`. Verify the commit diff is confined to `shared/`, `postprocessing/`, and `models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`.
6. **Push** `reigh-sprint-2` to `banodoco/Wan2GP` (confirm with user before pushing since this is visible-to-banodoco).
7. **Capture tip SHA** for the submodule pointer.

## Phase 2: Reigh-worker submodule cutover

### Step 5: Replace flat `Wan2GP/` tree with submodule (`reigh-worker/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Remove tracked flat tree**: `git rm -r --cached Wan2GP/` (removes from index, leaves working copy). Then `rm -rf Wan2GP/`.
2. **Add submodule**: `git submodule add -b reigh-sprint-2 https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git Wan2GP`. Creates `.gitmodules`, populates `.git/modules/Wan2GP/`, checks out the branch tip into `Wan2GP/`.
3. **Pin the submodule pointer** to the specific SHA from Step 4.7: `git -C Wan2GP checkout <sha>` then `git add Wan2GP .gitmodules`.
4. **Verify `.gitmodules`**:
   ```
   [submodule "Wan2GP"]
       path = Wan2GP
       url = https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git
       branch = reigh-sprint-2
   ```
5. **Verify submodule health**: `git submodule status` shows `Wan2GP` at the pinned SHA with no `+`/`-` prefix.
6. **Do not commit yet** — run validation gates first.

### Step 6: Validate path-contract smoke (strict path smoke)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the strict path-contract one-liner from the repo root:
   ```bash
   python3 -c "import os, sys; from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path, get_wan2gp_path; ensure_wan2gp_on_path(); assert os.path.abspath(str(get_wan2gp_path())) in [os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path]; assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py'); print('path-contract smoke OK')"
   ```
2. **Must exit 0** with `path-contract smoke OK`. If any assertion fails, pause cutover and diagnose (most likely: fork branch missing one of the five listed files).

### Step 7: Run the 6-file travel regression suite
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run**:
   ```bash
   uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py
   ```
2. **Must be all-green**. If any test fails, inspect whether the resynced `shared/` file touches code on the travel path. If so, reclassify the triggering file to bucket (c) on the fork branch (carry banodoco drift), amend Phase 1 Step 4, re-push, and re-pin.

## Phase 3: Doc updates

### Step 8: Rewrite stale vendored-subtree wording (`reigh-worker/README.md`, `reigh-worker/STRUCTURE.md`, `reigh-worker/docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`README.md:39`** — replace the "vendored upstream input ... subtree" sentence with submodule-accurate language describing pointer bumps, with guidance that upstream requirement changes are mirrored into root worker metadata before regenerating `uv.lock`.
2. **`README.md:103`** (Powered By section) — if present, correct "the `Wan2GP/` directory contains the upstream engine" to note the submodule pinned to `banodoco/Wan2GP`.
3. **`STRUCTURE.md:22`** — replace `vendored upstream. Dependency drift ...` with submodule-accurate language describing pointer bumps rather than subtree edits.
4. **`STRUCTURE.md:70`** — replace `Upstream video generation engine (vendored, DO NOT MODIFY)` with `Git submodule pointing at banodoco/Wan2GP; edit via fork PR + pointer bump, not in-place`.
5. **`docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`** — replace `still a vendored upstream subtree` with submodule language; preserve the paragraph's existing guidance about mirroring requirements into root worker metadata.

### Step 9: Doc-sweep gate (`reigh-worker/docs/`, `reigh-worker/README.md`, `reigh-worker/STRUCTURE.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Narrow sweep must return zero hits**:
   ```bash
   rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md
   ```
2. **Broader sweep must return zero hits**:
   ```bash
   rg -l 'Wan2GP' docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md | xargs -I{} rg -H -n 'DO NOT MODIFY' {}
   ```
3. Note: hits on `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md` for the first regex are historical prose describing the pre-Sprint-2 state; those are expected to remain (the plan doc is not among the stale-doc update targets and its narrative is about what *was* true). Confirm with the reviewer that those hits are acceptable — if not, reword those prose lines.

## Phase 4: Land

### Step 10: Commit the submodule cutover + doc updates
**Scope:** Small
1. **Stage**: `.gitmodules`, `Wan2GP` (the gitlink), all deletions under `Wan2GP/`, `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`.
2. **Commit** with a message like `chore: replace vendored Wan2GP tree with submodule at reigh-sprint-2`. Reference the baseline tag in the body.
3. **Do not push without explicit user confirmation** — the submodule commit permanently alters the repo shape.

## Execution Order

1. Preflight and baseline tag first (Step 1) — this is the rollback anchor.
2. Build the fork branch completely offline in a scratch clone (Steps 2–4), push only after local sanity checks.
3. Cut reigh-worker over to the submodule (Step 5), then gate on the strict path smoke (Step 6) BEFORE running pytest.
4. Run the 6-file travel suite (Step 7).
5. Update docs (Step 8) and gate doc-sweep (Step 9).
6. Commit (Step 10).

## Validation Order

1. Cheapest: strict path-contract smoke (Step 6) — instant feedback on the SD-001 invariant.
2. Fast: doc-sweep regex sweeps (Step 9).
3. Slowest: 6-file travel pytest suite (Step 7).

If any of (A) strict path smoke, (B) 6-file suite, (C) doc-sweep gate fails, roll back:
```bash
git submodule deinit Wan2GP && git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline
```
Rollback leaves the banodoco `reigh-sprint-2` branch in place (no harm); push can be retried after fixing the failing gate.

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.
